<style>
    /* This ensures the shipping container is always visible.
      HighLevel might add 'display: none' to this element,
      so '!important' is used to override it.
    */
    .shipping-container {
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* This is the placeholder message/button that shows when no shipping rates are available.
      It's styled to be noticeable and guide the user.
    */
    #shipping-placeholder {
        display: none; /* Hidden by default */
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px dashed #dee2e6;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-top: 15px;
        color: #495057;
    }

    #shipping-placeholder.visible {
        display: block; /* Made visible by JavaScript */
    }

    #shipping-placeholder p {
        margin: 0 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* This is the button that appears within the placeholder.
      It doesn't actually fetch rates but prompts the user to complete their address,
      which is the action that triggers HighLevel's rate fetch.
    */
    #fetch-shipping-btn {
        background-color: #007bff;
        color: white;
        border: none;
        padding: 10px 20px;

    }

    */
    .payment-container-relative {
        position: relative;
    }

    /* This is the overlay that disables the payment section.

    #payment-overlay {

<style>
    /* This ensures the shipping container is always visible.
      HighLevel might add 'display: none' to this element,
      so '!important' is used to override it.
    */
    .shipping-container {
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* This is the placeholder message/button that shows when no shipping rates are available.
      It's styled to be noticeable and guide the user.
    */
    #shipping-placeholder {
        display: none; /* Hidden by default */
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px dashed #dee2e6;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-top: 15px;
        color: #495057;
    }

    #shipping-placeholder.visible {
        display: block; /* Made visible by JavaScript */
    }

    #shipping-placeholder p {
        margin: 0 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* This is the button that appears within the placeholder.
      It doesn't actually fetch rates but prompts the user to complete their address,
      which is the action that triggers HighLevel's rate fetch.
    */
    #fetch-shipping-btn {
        background-color: #007bff;
        color: white;
        border: none;
        padding: 10px 20px;
        border-radius: 5px;
        cursor: pointer;
        font-weight: bold;
        transition: background-color 0.3s ease;
    }

    #fetch-shipping-btn:hover {
        background-color: #0056b3;
    }

    /* This class is added by JS to the payment container to prepare it for the overlay.
      'position: relative' is crucial for the absolute positioning of the child overlay.
    */
    .payment-container-relative {
        position: relative;
    }

    /* This is the overlay that disables the payment section.
      It has a semi-transparent background, a message, and a lock icon.
    */
    #payment-overlay {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
        backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        z-index: 10;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        text-align: center;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 20px;
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease, visibility 0.3s ease;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        cursor: pointer; /* V1.2: Make overlay clickable */
    }

    #payment-overlay.visible {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
    }

    /* Styles for the text and icon inside the payment overlay */
    #payment-overlay .overlay-content {
        color: #343a40;

    }

<style>
    /* This ensures the shipping container is always visible.
      HighLevel might add 'display: none' to this element,
      so '!important' is used to override it.
    */
    .shipping-container {
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* This is the placeholder message/button that shows when no shipping rates are available.
      It's styled to be noticeable and guide the user.
    */
    #shipping-placeholder {
        display: none; /* Hidden by default */
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px dashed #dee2e6;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-top: 15px;
        color: #495057;
    }

    #shipping-placeholder.visible {
        display: block; /* Made visible by JavaScript */
    }

    #shipping-placeholder p {
        margin: 0 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* This is the button that appears within the placeholder.
      It doesn't actually fetch rates but prompts the user to complete their address,
      which is the action that triggers HighLevel's rate fetch.
    */
    #fetch-shipping-btn {
        background-color: #007bff;
        color: white;
        border: none;
        padding: 10px 20px;

    }

    */
    .payment-container-relative {
        position: relative;
    }

    /* This is the overlay that disables the payment section.

    #payment-overlay {

<style>
    /* This ensures the shipping container is always visible.
      HighLevel might add 'display: none' to this element,
      so '!important' is used to override it.
    */
    .shipping-container {
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* This is the placeholder message/button that shows when no shipping rates are available.
      It's styled to be noticeable and guide the user.
    */
    #shipping-placeholder {
        display: none; /* Hidden by default */
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px dashed #dee2e6;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-top: 15px;
        color: #495057;
    }

    #shipping-placeholder.visible {
        display: block; /* Made visible by JavaScript */
    }

    #shipping-placeholder p {
        margin: 0 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* This is the button that appears within the placeholder.
      It doesn't actually fetch rates but prompts the user to complete their address,
      which is the action that triggers HighLevel's rate fetch.
    */
    #fetch-shipping-btn {
        background-color: #007bff;
        color: white;
        border: none;

    }

    }

    }

    /* This is the overlay that disables the payment section.

<style>
    /* This ensures the shipping container is always visible.
      HighLevel might add 'display: none' to this element,
      so '!important' is used to override it.
    */
    .shipping-container {
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* This is the placeholder message/button that shows when no shipping rates are available.
      It's styled to be noticeable and guide the user.
    */
    #shipping-placeholder {
        display: none; /* Hidden by default */
        padding: 20px;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: #f8f9fa;
        border: 2px dashed #dee2e6;
        border-radius: 8px;
        margin-top: 15px;
        color: #495057;
    }

    #shipping-placeholder.visible {
        display: block; /* Made visible by JavaScript */
    }

    #shipping-placeholder p {
        margin: 0 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* This is the button that appears within the placeholder.
      It doesn't actually fetch rates but prompts the user to complete their address,
      which is the action that triggers HighLevel's rate fetch.
    */
    #fetch-shipping-btn {
        background-color: #007bff;
        color: white;
        border: none;
        padding: 10px 20px;
        border-radius: 5px;
        cursor: pointer;
        font-weight: bold;
        transition: background-color 0.3s ease;
    }

    #fetch-shipping-btn:hover {
        background-color: #0056b3;
    }

    /* This class is added by JS to the payment container to prepare it for the overlay.
      'position: relative' is crucial for the absolute positioning of the child overlay.
    */
    .payment-container-relative {
        position: relative;
    }

    /* This is the overlay that disables the payment section.
      It has a semi-transparent background, a message, and a lock icon.
    */
    #payment-overlay {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
        backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        z-index: 10;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        text-align: center;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 20px;
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease, visibility 0.3s ease;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        cursor: pointer; /* V1.2: Make overlay clickable */
    }

    #payment-overlay.visible {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
    }

    /* Styles for the text and icon inside the payment overlay */
